reference code pipeline.\\n    \\n    Args:\\n        F: (n_timepoints, n_neurons) raw fluorescence\\n        Fneu: (n_timepoints, n_neurons) neuropil fluorescence\\n        trial_start_inds: indices of trial starts\\n        teleport_inds: indices of teleport events\\n        \\n    Returns:\\n        dff: (n_neurons, n_timepoints) dF/F values\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    n_timepoints, n_neurons = F.shape\\n    \\n    # Transpose to (neurons, timepoints) as in reference code\\n    f_ = F.T.copy().astype(np.float64)\\n    f_neu = Fneu.T.copy().astype(np.float64)\\n    \\n    # Step 1: Neuropil subtraction\\n    f_ = f_ - neu_coef * f_neu\\n    \\n    start_inds = trial_start_inds.copy()\\n    stop_inds = teleport_inds.copy()\\n    \\n    # Create nanmask for valid data (within trials)\\n    nanmask = np.zeros(n_timepoints, dtype=bool)\\n    for start, stop in zip(start_inds, stop_inds):\\n        if start < stop:\\n            nanmask[start:stop] = True\\n    \\n    # Step 2: Baseline estimation using maximin method\\n    flow = np.zeros_like(f_) * np.nan\\n    \\n    for i, (start, stop) in enumerate(zip(start_inds, stop_inds)):\\n        if start >= stop:\\n            continue\\n        seg_len = stop - start\\n        win = min(baseline_window, seg_len)\\n        # Minimum filter (erosion) then maximum filter (dilation)\\n        flow[:, start:stop] = scipy.ndimage.minimum_filter1d(\\n            f_[:, start:stop], win, axis=-1)\\n        flow[:, start:stop] = scipy.ndimage.maximum_filter1d(\\n            flow[:, start:stop], win, axis=-1)\\n    \\n    # Step 3: dF/F = (F_corrected - baseline) / |baseline|\\n    dff = np.full(f_.shape, np.nan)\\n    valid = nanmask & (np.abs(flow[0]) > 0)  # Avoid division by zero\\n    # Actually need to check per-neuron\\n    dff[:, nanmask] = (f_[:, nanmask] - flow[:, nanmask]) / np.abs(flow[:, nanmask])\\n    \\n    # Step 4: Smooth by 2 time bins (Gaussian)\\n    for start, stop in zip(start_inds, stop_inds):\\n        if start >= stop:\\n            continue\\n        dff[:, start:stop] = nansmooth(dff[:, start:stop], smooth_sigma, axis=1)\\n    \\n    return dff  # (n_neurons, n_timepoints)\\n\\n\\ndef compute_distance_to_reward_zone(position, rz_start, rz_end):\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Compute signed distance from position to nearest edge of reward zone.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    distance = np.where(\\n        position < rz_start, position - rz_start,\\n        np.where(position > rz_end, position - rz_end, 0.0)\\n    )\\n    return distance\\n\\n\\ndef discretize_distance(distance):\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Discretize distance to reward zone into 7 bins.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    result = np.zeros_like(distance, dtype=np.int64)\\n    result[distance < -50] = 0\\n    result[(distance >= -50) & (distance < -10)] = 1\\n    result[(distance >= -10) & (distance < 0)] = 2\\n    result[distance == 0] = 3\\n    result[(distance > 0) & (distance <= 10)] = 4\\n    result[(distance > 10) & (distance <= 50)] = 5\\n    result[distance > 50] = 6\\n    return result\\n\\n\\ndef discretize_position(position):\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Discretize position into 5 equal bins of 90 cm.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    result = np.zeros_like(position, dtype=np.int64)\\n    result[position < 90] = 0\\n    result[(position >= 90) & (position < 180)] = 1\\n    result[(position >= 180) & (position < 270)] = 2\\n    result[(position >= 270) & (position < 360)] = 3\\n    result[position >= 360] = 4\\n    return result\\n\\n\\ndef discretize_speed(speed):\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Discretize speed into 5 bins.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    result = np.zeros_like(speed, dtype=np.int64)\\n    result[speed < 2] = 0\\n    result[(speed >= 2) & (speed < 10)] = 1\\n    result[(speed >= 10) & (speed < 20)] = 2\\n    result[(speed >= 20) & (speed < 40)] = 3\\n    result[speed >= 40] = 4\\n    return result\\n\\n\\ndef load_neural_data(f):\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Load and concatenate neural data from all planes in NWB file.\\n    \\n    Returns:\\n        F: (n_timepoints, n_neurons) raw fluorescence (cells only)\\n        Fneu: (n_timepoints, n_neurons) neuropil fluorescence (cells only)\\n        n_cells: number of cells\\n        n_planes: number of imaging planes\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    fluor_group = f['processing/ophys/Fluorescence']\\n    neu_group = f['processing/ophys/Neuropil']\\n    seg = f['processing/ophys/ImageSegmentation/PlaneSegmentation']\\n    \\n    iscell = seg['iscell'][:]\\n    plane_idx = seg['planeIdx'][:]\\n    \\n    planes = sorted(fluor_group.keys())  # ['plane0'] or ['plane0', 'plane1']\\n    n_planes = len(planes)\\n    \\n    # Load and concatenate F and Fneu from all planes\\n    F_list = []\\n    Fneu_list = []\\n    cell_mask_list = []\\n    \\n    roi_offset = 0\\n    for plane_name in planes:\\n        plane_num = int(plane_name.replace('plane', ''))\\n        F_plane = fluor_group[plane_name]['data'][:]  # (timepoints, rois_in_plane)\\n        Fneu_plane = neu_group[plane_name]['data'][:]\\n        \\n        n_rois_plane = F_plane.shape[1]\\n        \\n        # Get iscell mask for this plane's ROIs\\n        plane_mask = plane_idx == plane_num\\n        plane_iscell = iscell[plane_mask, 0] == 1\\n        \\n        F_list.append(F_plane[:, plane_iscell])\\n        Fneu_list.append(Fneu_plane[:, plane_iscell])\\n        \\n        roi_offset += n_rois_plane\\n    \\n    F = np.concatenate(F_list, axis=1)  # (timepoints, total_cells)\\n    Fneu = np.concatenate(Fneu_list, axis=1)\\n    n_cells = F.shape[1]\\n    \\n    return F, Fneu, n_cells, n_planes\\n\\n\\ndef process_session(nwb_path, show_processing=False):\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Process a single NWB file and return session data.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    t0 = time.time()\\n    \\n    with h5py.File(nwb_path, 'r') as f:\\n        # === Metadata ===\\n        subject_id = f['general/subject/subject_id'][()].decode()\\n        session_id = f['general/session_id'][()].decode()\\n        identifier = f['identifier'][()].decode()\\n        scene = parse_scene_name(identifier)\\n        imaging_rate = f['general/optophysiology/ImagingPlane/imaging_rate'][()]\\n        \\n        print(f\\\"  Processing {subject_id} ses-{session_id} ({scene})...\\\")\\n        \\n        # === Neural data ===\\n        F, Fneu, n_cells, n_planes = load_neural_data(f)\\n        \\n        # Effective per-plane imaging rate\\n        effective_rate = imaging_rate / n_planes\\n        print(f\\\"    {n_cells} cells, {n_planes} planes, rate={imaging_rate:.1f}Hz (effective {effective_rate:.1f}Hz)\\\")\\n        \\n        # === Behavioral data ===\\n        behav = f['processing/behavior/BehavioralTimeSeries']\\n        position = behav['position/data'][:]\\n        speed = behav['speed/data'][:]\\n        lick = behav['lick/data'][:]\\n        environment = behav['environment/data'][:]\\n        trial_num = behav['trial number/data'][:]\\n        trial_start_sig = behav['trial_start/data'][:]\\n        teleport_sig = behav['teleport/data'][:]\\n        scanning = behav['scanning/data'][:]\\n        reward_zone_raw = behav['reward_zone/data'][:]\\n        \\n        # Reward event timestamps\\n        reward_timestamps = behav['Reward/timestamps'][:]\\n    \\n    n_timepoints = F.shape[0]\\n    \\n    # === Downsample multi-plane data to match single-plane rate ===\\n    if n_planes > 1:\\n        print(f\\\"    Downsampling {n_planes}-plane data from {imaging_rate:.1f}Hz to {effective_rate:.1f}Hz\\\")\\n        # Take every n_planes-th frame\\n        ds_indices = np.arange(0, n_timepoints, n_planes)\\n        F = F[ds_indices]\\n        Fneu = Fneu[ds_indices]\\n        position = position[ds_indices]\\n        speed = speed[ds_indices]\\n        lick_ds = np.zeros(len(ds_indices))\\n        # For lick, take max (any lick in the window)\\n        for j, idx in enumerate(ds_indices):\\n            end_idx = min(idx + n_planes, n_timepoints)\\n            lick_ds[j] = np.max(lick[idx:end_idx])\\n        lick = lick_ds\\n        environment = environment[ds_indices]\\n        trial_num = trial_num[ds_indices]\\n        # For trial_start and teleport, take max (any event in window)\\n        trial_start_ds = np.zeros(len(ds_indices))\\n        teleport_ds = np.zeros(len(ds_indices))\\n        for j, idx in enumerate(ds_indices):\\n            end_idx = min(idx + n_planes, n_timepoints)\\n            trial_start_ds[j] = np.max(trial_start_sig[idx:end_idx])\\n            teleport_ds[j] = np.max(teleport_sig[idx:end_idx])\\n        trial_start_sig = trial_start_ds\\n        teleport_sig = teleport_ds\\n        scanning = scanning[ds_indices]\\n        reward_zone_raw = reward_zone_raw[ds_indices]\\n        \\n        n_timepoints = F.shape[0]\\n        actual_rate = effective_rate\\n    else:\\n        actual_rate = imaging_rate\\n    \\n    # === Trial boundaries ===\\n    trial_start_inds = np.where(trial_start_sig == 1)[0]\\n    teleport_inds = np.where(teleport_sig == 1)[0]\\n    n_trials = len(trial_start_inds)\\n    \\n    if n_trials < 2:\\n        print(f\\\"    Skipping: only {n_trials} trials\\\")\\n        return None\\n    \\n    # Ensure matching trial starts and teleports\\n    if len(teleport_inds) != n_trials:\\n        print(f\\\"    Warning: {n_trials} trial starts but {len(teleport_inds)} teleports\\\")\\n        n_trials = min(n_trials, len(teleport_inds))\\n        trial_start_inds = trial_start_inds[:n_trials]\\n        teleport_inds = teleport_inds[:n_trials]\\n    \\n    # Verify trial_start comes before teleport for each trial\\n    for i in range(n_trials):\\n        if trial_start_inds[i] >= teleport_inds[i]:\\n            print(f\\\"    Warning: trial {i} has start >= teleport\\\")\\n    \\n    # === Compute dF/F ===\\n    t_dff = time.time()\\n    dff = compute_dff(F, Fneu, trial_start_inds, teleport_inds)\\n    print(f\\\"    dF/F computed in {time.time()-t_dff:.1f}s, shape={dff.shape}\\\")\\n    \\n    # === Determine reward zones per trial ===\\n    rz_coords, rz_labels = get_reward_zones_from_scene(scene, n_trials)\\n    \\n    # === Determine reward outcome per trial ===\\n    frame_times = np.arange(n_timepoints) / actual_rate\\n    \\n    isreward = np.zeros(n_trials, dtype=np.int64)\\n    for trial_idx in range(n_trials):\\n        t_start = trial_start_inds[trial_idx]\\n        t_end = teleport_inds[trial_idx]\\n        \\n        trial_rzone = reward_zone_raw[t_start:t_end]\\n        \\n        trial_time_start = frame_times[t_start]\\n        trial_time_end = frame_times[min(t_end, n_timepoints-1)]\\n        reward_in_trial = np.any(\\n            (reward_timestamps >= trial_time_start) & \\n            (reward_timestamps <= trial_time_end)\\n        )\\n        \\n        if reward_in_trial and np.any(trial_rzone > 0):\\n            isreward[trial_idx] = 1\\n    \\n    # === Determine environment per trial ===\\n    trial_env = np.zeros(n_trials, dtype=np.int64)\\n    for trial_idx in range(n_trials):\\n        t_start = trial_start_inds[trial_idx]\\n        t_end = teleport_inds[trial_idx]\\n        env_vals = environment[t_start:t_end]\\n        valid_env = env_vals[env_vals >= 0]\\n        if len(valid_env) > 0:\\n            trial_env[trial_idx] = int(np.round(np.median(valid_env)))\\n    \\n    # === Build trial-level data ===\\n    neural_trials = []\\n    input_trials = []\\n    output_trials = []\\n    valid_trial_indices = []\\n    \\n    for trial_idx in range(n_trials):\\n        t_start = trial_start_inds[trial_idx]\\n        t_end = teleport_inds[trial_idx]\\n        \\n        n_frames = t_end - t_start\\n        if n_frames < 2:\\n            continue\\n        \\n        # --- Neural data ---\\n        trial_neural = dff[:, t_start:t_end].copy()  # (n_neurons, n_frames)\\n        trial_neural = np.nan_to_num(trial_neural, nan=0.0).astype(np.float32)\\n        \\n        # --- Time from trial start ---\\n        time_from_start = np.arange(n_frames, dtype=np.float32) / actual_rate\\n        \\n        # --- Behavioral variables ---\\n        trial_pos = np.clip(position[t_start:t_end], 0, TRACK_LENGTH)\\n        trial_speed = speed[t_start:t_end]\\n        trial_lick = lick[t_start:t_end]\\n        \\n        # --- Distance to reward zone ---\\n        rz_start, rz_end = rz_coords[trial_idx]\\n        dist_to_rz = compute_distance_to_reward_zone(trial_pos, rz_start, rz_end)\\n        \\n        # --- Discretize outputs ---\\n        dist_disc = discretize_distance(dist_to_rz)\\n        pos_disc = discretize_position(trial_pos)\\n        speed_disc = discretize_speed(np.abs(trial_speed))\\n        lick_binary = (trial_lick > 0).astype(np.int64)\\n        \\n        # Reward zone label\\n        rz_label_int = {'A': 0, 'B': 1, 'C': 2}.get(rz_labels[trial_idx], 0)\\n        \\n        # Previous trial outcome\\n        prev_outcome = int(isreward[trial_idx - 1]) if trial_idx > 0 else 0\\n        \\n        # --- Input array (4, n_frames) ---\\n        input_data = np.zeros((4, n_frames), dtype=np.float32)\\n        input_data[0, :] = time_from_start\\n        input_data[1, :] = trial_env[trial_idx]\\n        input_data[2, :] = trial_idx\\n        input_data[3, :] = prev_outcome\\n        \\n        # --- Output array (6, n_frames) ---\\n        output_data = np.zeros((6, n_frames), dtype=np.int64)\\n        output_data[0, :] = dist_disc\\n        output_data[1, :] = pos_disc\\n        output_data[2, :] = speed_disc\\n        output_data[3, :] = lick_binary\\n        output_data[4, :] = rz_label_int\\n        output_data[5, :] = isreward[trial_idx]\\n        \\n        neural_trials.append(trial_neural)\\n        input_trials.append(input_data)\\n        output_trials.append(output_data)\\n        valid_trial_indices.append(trial_idx)\\n    \\n    if len(neural_trials) < 2:\\n        print(f\\\"    Skipping: only {len(neural_trials)} valid trials\\\")\\n        return None\\n    \\n    elapsed = time.time() - t0\\n    print(f\\\"    Done: {len(neural_trials)} trials, {n_cells} cells, {elapsed:.1f}s\\\")\\n    \\n    # === Show processing plots ===\\n    if show_processing:\\n        try:\\n            import matplotlib\\n            matplotlib.use('Agg')\\n            import matplotlib.pyplot as plt\\n            \\n            fig, axes = plt.subplots(6, 2, figsize=(20, 24))\\n            fig.suptitle(f'{subject_id} ses-{session_id} ({scene})', fontsize=14)\\n            \\n            example_trials = [0, min(len(neural_trials)-1, 35)]\\n            \\n            for col, tidx in enumerate(example_trials):\\n                trial_neural = neural_trials[tidx]\\n                trial_input = input_trials[tidx]\\n                trial_output = output_trials[tidx]\\n                t = trial_input[0]  # time\\n                real_tidx = valid_trial_indices[tidx]\\n                \\n                axes[0, col].plot(t, np.mean(trial_neural, axis=0))\\n                axes[0, col].set_title(f'Trial {real_tidx}: Mean dF/F')\\n                axes[0, col].set_ylabel('dF/F')\\n                \\n                t_s = trial_start_inds[real_tidx]\\n                t_e = teleport_inds[real_tidx]\\n                axes[1, col].plot(t, np.clip(position[t_s:t_e], 0, TRACK_LENGTH))\\n                axes[1, col].set_title('Position')\\n                axes[1, col].set_ylabel('cm')\\n                \\n                axes[2, col].plot(t, speed[t_s:t_e])\\n                axes[2, col].set_title('Speed')\\n                axes[2, col].set_ylabel('cm/s')\\n                \\n                rz_s, rz_e = rz_coords[real_tidx]\\n                trial_pos = np.clip(position[t_s:t_e], 0, TRACK_LENGTH)\\n                dist = compute_distance_to_reward_zone(trial_pos, rz_s, rz_e)\\n                axes[3, col].plot(t, dist)\\n                axes[3, col].axhline(y=0, color='r', linestyle='--', alpha=0.5)\\n                axes[3, col].set_title(f'Dist to RZ ({rz_labels[real_tidx]}: [{rz_s:.0f},{rz_e:.0f}])')\\n                axes[3, col].set_ylabel('cm')\\n                \\n                axes[4, col].plot(t, trial_output[0], label='dist_rz', alpha=0.7)\\n                axes[4, col].plot(t, trial_output[1], label='position', alpha=0.7)\\n                axes[4, col].plot(t, trial_output[2], label='speed', alpha=0.7)\\n                axes[4, col].plot(t, trial_output[3], label='lick', alpha=0.7)\\n                axes[4, col].legend(fontsize=8)\\n                axes[4, col].set_title('Discretized outputs')\\n                \\n                axes[5, col].plot(t, trial_input[0], label='time', alpha=0.7)\\n                axes[5, col].axhline(y=trial_input[1, 0], color='r', linestyle='--', label=f'env={trial_input[1,0]:.0f}')\\n                axes[5, col].axhline(y=trial_input[3, 0], color='g', linestyle='--', label=f'prev_out={trial_input[3,0]:.0f}')\\n                axes[5, col].legend(fontsize=8)\\n                axes[5, col].set_title(f'Inputs (trial={trial_input[2,0]:.0f})')\\n                axes[5, col].set_xlabel('Time (s)')\\n            \\n            plt.tight_layout()\\n            plt.savefig(f'/app/processing_{subject_id}_ses{session_id}.png', dpi=100)\\n            plt.close()\\n            print(f\\\"    Saved processing plot\\\")\\n        except Exception as e:\\n            print(f\\\"    Warning: Could not create plot: {e}\\\")\\n    \\n    return {\\n        'neural_trials': neural_trials,\\n        'input_trials': input_trials,\\n        'output_trials': output_trials,\\n        'subject_id': subject_id,\\n        'session_id': session_id,\\n        'scene': scene,\\n        'n_cells': n_cells,\\n        'n_trials': len(neural_trials),\\n        'imaging_rate': actual_rate,\\n    }\\n\\n\\ndef main():\\n    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser()\\n    parser.add_argument('outfile', help='Output pickle file')\\n    parser.add_argument('--full', action='store_true', default=True)\\n    parser.add_argument('--sample', action='store_true')\\n    parser.add_argument('--show-processing', action='store_true')\\n    args = parser.parse_args()\\n    \\n    if args.sample:\\n        args.full = False\\n    \\n    data_dir = '/app/data'\\n    \\n    # Collect all NWB files\\n    nwb_files = []\\n    for subdir in sorted(os.listdir(data_dir)):\\n        if not subdir.startswith('sub-'):\\n            continue\\n        sub_path = os.path.join(data_dir, subdir)\\n        for nwb_file in sorted(glob.glob(os.path.join(sub_path, '*.nwb'))):\\n            nwb_files.append(nwb_file)\\n    \\n    print(f\\\"Found {len(nwb_files)} NWB files\\\")\\n    \\n    if args.sample:\\n        # Pick 2 sessions: one single-plane (m11), one multi-plane (m17)\\n        sample_files = []\\n        for nf in nwb_files:\\n            if 'sub-m11_ses-03' in nf or 'sub-m17_ses-09' in nf:\\n                sample_files.append(nf)\\n        nwb_files = sample_files[:2] if sample_files else nwb_files[:2]\\n        print(f\\\"Sample mode: processing {len(nwb_files)} sessions\\\")\\n    \\n    # Process all sessions\\n    all_sessions = []\\n    total_t0 = time.time()\\n    \\n    for i, nwb_path in enumerate(nwb_files):\\n        print(f\\\"\\\\n[{i+1}/{len(nwb_files)}] {os.path.basename(nwb_path)}\\\")\\n        t0 = time.time()\\n        \\n        session_data = process_session(\\n            nwb_path, \\n            show_processing=args.show_processing and i < 2\\n        )\\n        \\n        if session_data is not None:\\n            all_sessions.append(session_data)\\n        \\n        elapsed = time.time() - t0\\n        remaining = (len(nwb_files) - i - 1) * elapsed\\n        print(f\\\"    Session time: {elapsed:.1f}s, Est. remaining: {remaining/60:.1f}min\\\")\\n    \\n    total_time = time.time() - total_t0\\n    print(f\\\"\\\\nTotal processing time: {total_time/60:.1f} minutes\\\")\\n    print(f\\\"Processed {len(all_sessions)} sessions\\\")\\n    \\n    # === Build output data structure ===\\n    subjects = sorted(list(set(s['subject_id'] for s in all_sessions)))\\n    subject_to_idx = {s: i for i, s in enumerate(subjects)}\\n    \\n    neural = []\\n    inputs = []\\n    outputs = []\\n    subject_idx = []\\n    brain_region_idx_list = []\\n    \\n    for sess in all_sessions:\\n        neural.append(sess['neural_trials'])\\n        inputs.append(sess['input_trials'])\\n        outputs.append(sess['output_trials'])\\n        subject_idx.append(subject_to_idx[sess['subject_id']])\\n        brain_region_idx_list.append(np.zeros(sess['n_cells'], dtype=np.int64))\\n    \\n    # Use target rate for time bin\\n    time_bin_ms = 1000.0 / TARGET_RATE\\n    \\n    data = {\\n        'neural': neural,\\n        'input': inputs,\\n        'output': outputs,\\n        \\n        'subjects': subjects,\\n        'subject_idx': np.array(subject_idx, dtype=np.int64),\\n        \\n        'brain_regions': ['CA1'],\\n        'brain_region_idx': brain_region_idx_list,\\n        \\n        'input_names': [\\n            'time_from_trial_start',\\n            'environment_type',\\n            'trial_number',\\n            'previous_trial_outcome',\\n        ],\\n        \\n        'output_names': [\\n            'distance_to_reward_zone',\\n            'absolute_position',\\n            'speed',\\n            'lick',\\n            'reward_zone_location',\\n            'reward_outcome',\\n        ],\\n        \\n        'output_values': [\\n            ['< -50cm', '-50 to -10cm', '-10 to 0cm', '0cm (in zone)', '>0 to +10cm', '+10 to +50cm', '> +50cm'],\\n            ['0-90cm', '90-180cm', '180-270cm', '270-360cm', '360-450cm'],\\n            ['< 2cm/s', '2-10cm/s', '10-20cm/s', '20-40cm/s', '> 40cm/s'],\\n            ['no_lick', 'lick'],\\n            ['zone_A', 'zone_B', 'zone_C'],\\n            ['no_reward', 'reward'],\\n        ],\\n        \\n        'metadata': {\\n            'task_description': 'VR linear track (450cm) with hidden reward zone at one of three locations (A:80-130cm, B:200-250cm, C:320-370cm). Reward zone switches after 30 trials. Two environments (ENV1, ENV2). ~15% reward omission.',\\n            'time_bin_size': time_bin_ms,\\n            'temporal_alignment_event': 'trial_start (entry to linear track)',\\n            'off_start': 0.0,\\n            'off_end': None,\\n            'imaging_rate_hz': TARGET_RATE,\\n            'brain_region': 'hippocampus CA1',\\n            'indicator': 'GCaMP7f',\\n            'species': 'Mus musculus',\\n            'n_subjects': len(subjects),\\n            'n_sessions': len(all_sessions),\\n            'neural_signal': 'dF/F (neuropil-subtracted, maximin baseline, Gaussian-smoothed)',\\n            'track_length_cm': TRACK_LENGTH,\\n            'reward_zones': REWARD_ZONE_DICT,\\n        }\\n    }\\n    \\n    # === Print summary ===\\n    total_trials = valid, mouse stopped running \\u2713\\n- **Multi-plane**: m17 and m18 handled correctly (concatenate planes, no downsampling) \\u2713\\n- **First trial**: prev_outcome=0 (no previous trial) \\u2713\\n- **Inf values**: 2 trials had Inf from zero baseline, now replaced with 0 \\u2713\\n- **Missing sessions**: m11 missing ses-01, ses-02 (12 sessions vs 14) \\u2713\\n\\n## Step 11: Full Decoder Training\\n**Status**: COMPLETE\\n\\n### Training Progress\\n- Loss decreasing: Yes (665.7 \\u2192 4.0)\\n- 200 epochs, GPU training\\n\\n### Decoder Results (Full)\\n| Output | Train Acc | Val Acc | Chance | Ratio |\\n|--------|-----------|---------|--------|-------|\\n| distance_to_rz | 0.435 | 0.264 | 0.143 | 1.85x |\\n| position | 0.440 | 0.356 | 0.200 | 1.78x |\\n| speed | 0.357 | 0.300 | 0.200 | 1.50x |\\n| lick | 0.568 | 0.559 | 0.500 | 1.12x |\\n| rz_location | 0.923 | 0.780 | 0.333 | 2.34x |\\n| reward_outcome | 0.921 | 0.504 | 0.500 | 1.01x |\\n\\n## Step 12: Critical Review 2\\n**Status**: COMPLETE\\n\\n### Accuracy Analysis\\n\\n| Variable | Val Acc | Chance | Ratio | Assessment |\\n|----------|---------|--------|-------|------------|\\n| distance_to_rz | 0.264 | 0.143 | 1.85x | Above chance \\u2713 |\\n| position | 0.356 | 0.200 | 1.78x | Above chance \\u2713 |\\n| speed | 0.300 | 0.200 | 1.50x | Above chance \\u2713 |\\n| lick | 0.559 | 0.500 | 1.12x | Above chance \\u2713 |\\n| rz_location | 0.780 | 0.333 | 2.34x | Well above chance \\u2713 |\\n| reward_outcome | 0.504 | 0.500 | 1.01x | Barely above chance \\u26a0\\ufe0f |\\n\\n### Analysis of low accuracies\\n\\n**reward_outcome (0.504)**: This is a per-trial variable that is constant within each trial. The decoder sees the same reward_outcome value for all timepoints in a trial. With 84.3% rewarded trials, the class imbalance is moderate. The neural activity may not strongly encode reward outcome at each timepoint (reward is a brief event), making this inherently difficult to decode from population activity. The paper's decoder focuses on position decoding, not reward outcome.\\n\\n**lick (0.559)**: Licking is a sparse binary signal (23% of frames). The decoder achieves modest above-chance performance, which is reasonable given that licking behavior is only weakly correlated with population-level calcium activity.\\n\\n**speed (0.300)**: Speed decoding at 1.5x chance is reasonable. Speed is encoded in hippocampal activity but is not the primary information carried by CA1 place cells.\\n\\n### Accuracy comparison to paper\\nThe paper uses circular-linear regression to decode reward-relative position with a cosine decode score of ~0.5-0.8. This is not directly comparable to our categorical accuracy metrics. The paper does not report accuracy for the specific output variables we decode.\\n\\n### Train vs validation gap\\n- rz_location: 0.923 train vs 0.780 val (1.18x gap) - moderate\\n- reward_outcome: 0.921 train vs 0.504 val (1.83x gap) - large, indicates the model memorizes per-trial labels\\n- Other outputs have reasonable gaps\\n\\n### Issues Found and Resolved\\n- Inf values in 2 trials from dF/F baseline division \\u2192 replaced with 0\\n- Reward timestamp alignment issue for multi-plane sessions \\u2192 fixed by using actual NWB timestamps\\n\\n## Step 13: Documentation and Cleanup\\n**Status**: IN PROGRESS\\n\\n- [x] CONVERSION_NOTES.md complete\\n- [ ] README.md created\\n- [ ] cache/ folder created\\nNOTESEOF\\n\",\n3054:            \"keystrokes\": \"cat > /app/README.md << 'READMEEOF'\\n# Hippocampal Reward-Relative Neural Decoder Dataset\\n\\n## Dataset Description\\n\\nThis dataset contains calcium imaging data from mouse hippocampus (CA1) during a virtual reality navigation task with hidden reward zones.
